Not verified (out of scope / no source given): the hardware anecdotes (2.4 GHz flakiness, 20-yard dropouts, 10-13 min battery), the Amazon listing, the researcher's own delay_loop2.py simulation numbers, and the 'f ~ 830 px for 720p/75 deg HFOV' inference (arithmetically consistent: 640/tan(37.5 deg) = 834). Two substantive corrections: X69 control loop defaults to 25 Hz not 80 Hz, and SIMC delay tolerance is +159% not +59%. Everything else held up against primary sources.

RECOMMENDATIONS:
- ARCHITECTURE: two-rate perception. Heavy detector (hosted sam-3.1 on single JPEG frames with the LLM-produced noun phrase, or local SAM 3.1 on MPS at ~1 fps) runs at 1-2 Hz for acquisition, verification and re-ID; a fast local tracker (EfficientTAM video predictor on MPS for a Meta-only stack, OpenCV VitTrack ~1.5 ms/frame as the pragmatic fallback) runs at camera rate (15-25 fps) seeded with the SAM box/mask; the controller runs at 10-20 Hz on the newest tracker output only. Do not put hosted SAM 3.1 video requests in the loop (clip-oriented, no streaming session, latency unpublished).
- FEATURES: use mask/box centroid x for yaw, log size error for pitch, and treat vertical error with suspicion. e_x = (cx/W - 0.5)*2; e_y = (cy/H - y_ref)*2 with y_ref ~0.42; e_s = ln(w_ref / w_box) using width (shoulder width for people, box width for vehicles) because pitching the vehicle shifts the image vertically by ~f*delta_pitch px (~145 px for 10 deg at 720p) but barely changes width. Smooth each with a first-order filter tau_F = theta_eff/2 (SIMC) and reject single-frame jumps (>25% of frame in position or >40% in size) as outliers.
- CONTROLLER (per axis, PD + feed-forward, no integrator on yaw): yaw = sat(Kp_y*e_x_pred + Kd_y*filtered d/dt(e_x) + Kff*v_target_x, +/-0.35); pitch = sat(Kp_p(Z)*deadband(e_s, +/-0.08), +/-0.25) with Kp_p scaled by clamp(w_ref/w_box, 0.5, 2.0) (1/Z sensitivity); throttle = sat(Kp_t*deadband(e_y_slow, +/-0.15), +/-0.15) with tau 1.0 s filter and muted while |pitch| > 0.10 (attitude coupling); roll = 0 in v1. Then slew-limit (<= 1.0 stick/s), saturate, expo 0, DirectStrategy, speed_index 0 (0.55 scale) while tuning. Use derivative-on-measurement and output-limit anti-windup (simple-pid semantics) and bumpless transfer (set_auto_mode(last_output)) when handing over from manual.
- GAINS FROM THE DELAY BUDGET: measure theta_eff = video glass-to-glass + command latency + half control period + attitude lag (expect ~0.6-0.8 s). Start P-only at loop gain Kp*K = 0.5/theta_eff (K = measured yaw rate per unit stick, rad/s; e_x in half-FOV radians), which in simulation gave ~4% overshoot and ~4 s settle; never exceed 0.7/theta_eff without a predictor (1.0/theta_eff gave 39% overshoot). Expect closed-loop bandwidth ~0.1 Hz: design the demo around slow targets and 3-5 m stand-off.
- LATENCY COMPENSATION (Smith-style own-motion predictor + ArduPilot-style target projection): timestamp frames at arrival, back-date by measured video delay, keep a ring buffer of commanded yaw/pitch, run an internal model (first-order lag tau_a ~0.25 s of cmd*K) and compute e_x_pred(t) = e_x(t_capture) - integral_{t_capture..t} yaw_rate_model dt / (HFOV/2) + v_target*(t - t_capture), where v_target comes from an alpha-beta / 2nd-order filter (PX4: wn 1 rad/s, zeta 0.707) on e_x. In simulation this allowed Kp*K of 1.2-1.6 (2x faster settle, <=3% overshoot) and tolerated a 30% delay-model error. Gate the predictor off (fall back to 0.5/theta_eff gains) whenever frame age exceeds 1.5x nominal.
- TARGET LIFECYCLE STATE MACHINE: IDLE -> (voice: muse-voice-transcribe -> muse-spark parses {noun_phrase, attributes, action}) -> ACQUIRE (SAM 3.1 on current frame with include_confidence; if >1 candidate, disambiguate by attribute, e.g. mean HSV of mask for 'blue shirt', or ask the LLM with a crop) -> CONFIRM (hover; require 3 consecutive frames / 1 s where local tracker IoU with SAM box > 0.5; show green box; DJI WaitingForConfirmation analogue) -> TRACK (tracker at camera rate; SAM verify every 1-2 s; re-seed tracker if IoU > 0.3; store appearance histogram) -> LOW_CONF (tracker score < 0.2 or outlier jump: zero sticks, immediate SAM re-query, match by IoU/appearance) -> LOST (no match 1.0 s: hover 2 s, then SEARCH yaw +/-0.15 stick toward last-seen side for max one 360 deg turn while re-querying SAM at 1-2 Hz) -> TIMEOUT (20-30 s: land, announce). Re-query cadence 1-2 Hz keeps hosted cost at ~$9-18/h and far below 3,000 RPM.
- SAFETY LAYER (separate thread with its own UDP socket, independent of plugin code): (1) dead-man: UI/keyboard heartbeat every 250 ms; none for 0.5 s (PX4 COM_RC_LOSS_T analogue) -> zero sticks, 2 s -> land flag 0x80; (2) video watchdog: newest frame age > 0.7 s -> zero sticks, > 2 s -> land; (3) caps: |pitch| <= 0.25, |yaw| <= 0.35, |throttle| <= 0.15, |roll| <= 0.2, slew <= 1 stick/s, speed_index 0; (4) geofence-by-time: dead-reckon forward displacement = sum(pitch_stick*K_v*dt); refuse further forward beyond ~5 m and beyond 60% of measured battery endurance (reports say 10-13 min; budget 6 min autonomy); (5) E-stop path: hotkey writes land (0x80) immediately and, only below ~1 m, emergency stop 0x02 (motor cut = fall); the safety thread builds its own packets via X69LgRcProtocolAdapter so it works even if the autonomy loop hangs; (6) operator override: any manual axis input (gamepad/keyboard) wins for 2 s using FlightController.set_axes_from source tags.
- DIGITAL TWIN: implement a drop-in pair (video adapter + RC protocol adapter, same seams as DebugVideoProtocolAdapter/X69LgRcProtocolAdapter) wrapping a kinematic quad model: stick -> first-order attitude lag (tau_a 0.25 s) -> acceleration -> velocity with drag -> position, yaw rate = K_yaw*stick, altitude-hold loop, optical-flow drift noise; pinhole camera with the real X69 HFOV, rendered in OpenCV/pyrender (or Blender frames) with moving person/car targets; inject video delay 200-500 ms, command delay 50-100 ms, 15-25 fps with jitter, 1-2 px box noise, 5% dropouts. Validate the twin by replaying the real step tests and matching rise time within 20%.
- SYSTEM-ID ON THE REAL DRONE (before any closed loop): glass-to-glass video latency with a phone stopwatch in view (also compare rtsp vs h265 modes); command latency via LED/prop-start; yaw step (0.3 stick for 2 s) -> K_yaw and tau_a from the image bearing trace; pitch step (0.25 for 1.5 s) -> size-growth gain; measure HFOV with a known-width object at a known distance.
- LOGGING AND METRICS (CSV/Parquet per control tick): wall time, frame timestamp and age, detection source (tracker/SAM), box, mask area, tracker score, SAM confidence and round-trip, e_x/e_y/e_s raw, filtered and predicted, P/D/FF terms, final sticks, state, battery, dropped frames. Report pixel-error RMS of e_x and e_s during TRACK, 2%-band settling time and % overshoot after a 30%-of-frame lateral target step (MathWorks stepinfo definitions), time-in-deadband %, lost events per minute, median re-acquire time, frame-age p50/p95, loop jitter, and twin-vs-real step overlays. Transfer rule: fly with gains at 60% of twin-optimal first, yaw only, then add pitch, then throttle/tilt.
- HACKATHON DEMO VISUALS: live feed with SAM mask overlay coloured by state (yellow candidate, green confirmed, red low-confidence), crosshair with deadband box, measured vs predicted centroid, stick bars, scrolling e_x/e_s traces with RMS, latency-budget bar (video, SAM round trip, command), state badge, voice transcript and parsed intent JSON, and a split screen of the twin and the real drone executing the same command.
- REPO FIXES TO MAKE FIRST: give the follow plugin a latest-only frame mailbox (bypass the 100-deep queue), feed it raw BGR frames instead of re-encoded JPEG, hold/decay sticks instead of snapping to zero on a single missed detection, and expose camera-tilt pulses (0.1-0.2 s on bits 0x08/0x10) as the preferred way to re-centre vertically instead of throttle.

RISKS:
- Effective loop delay ~0.6-0.8 s limits closed-loop bandwidth to ~0.1 Hz; a person walking at 1 m/s at 3 m (bearing rate ~19 deg/s) lags ~25 deg with P-only gains and can leave a ~75 deg FOV; demo must use slow targets, longer stand-off, and the predictor.
- Pitch-to-image coupling (no IMU access on the X69): forward acceleration shifts the target up by ~f*delta_pitch px, which a naive throttle loop will fight, producing altitude bobbing; vertical control must be slow, deadbanded, and muted during pitch.
- Hosted sam-3.1 latency is unpublished and object ids are not stable across exit/occlusion; local SAM 3.1 on Apple MPS relies on an unmerged PR (~1.3 fps, ~7 GB) and HF streaming mode produces more false positives/duplicates than offline mode.
- If hackathon rules require all perception from Meta models, OpenCV VitTrack is disqualified; keep a Meta-only mode (SAM 3.1 + EfficientTAM) and verify EfficientTAM fps on the M5 Pro early.
- Wi-Fi link: 2.4 GHz interference at a venue, app-reported dropouts at ~20 yards and flaky connects; the video watchdog will land the drone on stalls, so keep a recorded-video and twin fallback for the demo.
- turbodrone's 100-deep drop-oldest plugin queue silently adds seconds of latency when the consumer is slower than the camera; the H.265 UDP path needs buffering (nobuffer broke decoding), so RTSP latency should be measured rather than assumed.
- Emergency-stop flag 0x02 cuts motors (the drone falls); land (0x80) must be the default abort and stop reserved for near-ground use.
- Battery/brushed-motor sag (10-13 min reported) degrades altitude hold late in the pack; optical-flow drift over featureless floors appears as target motion the controller will chase.
- Hosted SAM cost is modest (~$9-18/h at 1-2 Hz) but 3,000 RPM is per team and shared with other teammates' testing.
- Most hardware anecdotes come from reviews of other 'Loiley' models and the Amazon title; X69 firmware behaviour on Wi-Fi loss or low battery is unverified and must be tested on the ground.

OPEN QUESTIONS FOR USER:
- Do the hackathon rules require every perception/tracking component to be a Meta model (then only SAM 3.1 + EfficientTAM are allowed in the loop), or may a non-Meta fast tracker like OpenCV VitTrack bridge between SAM re-detections?
- Will you use hosted sam-3.1 (per-image billing, unknown latency) or local SAM 3.1 on the M5 Pro via the MPS PR (~1.3 fps), or both with automatic fallback? Is there a budget cap for API calls during testing?
- Indoor or outdoor demo? Room size, ceiling height, floor texture (optical flow), and expected target speed (standing/slow walk vs. jogging; toy car speed)?
- Have you measured the X69's glass-to-glass video latency on your Mac in rtsp vs h265 mode, and the command latency? If not, can we schedule 15 minutes of ground tests before any control tuning?
- What is the operator setup: gamepad available for override, and are you willing to hold a dead-man key during autonomous flight?
- What does the X69 firmware do on Wi-Fi loss and low battery (hover, auto-land, nothing)? Can we test this on the ground with props removed?
- How faithful must the digital twin be: kinematic Python model with an OpenCV/pyrender-rendered camera (fast to build), or full physics plus Blender/photoreal rendering?
- Targets beyond people and cars (pets, bikes)? Should the drone also obey 'stop', 'come closer', 'back off', 'land' voice commands mid-track?
